- **memory-bench-pioneer**: Be one of the first to benchmark your agent's memory — and help shape how AI remembers. Runs a peer-review-grade evaluation suite (LLM-as-judge, nDCG/MAP/MRR with 95% CIs, ablation studies) against your live memory system and submits anonymized results to the ENGRAM/CORTEX research papers. Your data stays private; only aggregate stats leave. Works with agent-memory-ultimate. For the bold few who believe AI memory should be measured, not guessed at. Propose-only; it never writes to memory without your yes.
